Set the round trip to 150 ms or more, then find the loss that still lets one download reach 25 Mbps.		将往返时间设为至少 150 ms，再找出仍能让单次下载达到 25 Mbps 的丢包率。

game pings a minute for the games you watch (one a second per server while you play)		次游戏 ping/分钟，用于你关注的游戏（游戏时每台服务器每秒一次）
on top of the widget's once-a-second read of Windows' own counters.		此外，小组件每秒读取一次 Windows 自带的计数器。
The widget always stays light on the PC. The deep stuff (live capture, the full connection list, path checks) only runs while this window is open. These decide how much watching happens in the background - checks a minute, plus game pings once you watch a game.		小组件始终保持轻量。深入功能（实时抓包、完整连接列表、路径检查）仅在此窗口打开时运行。以下设置决定后台每分钟检查次数，以及关注游戏后额外进行的游戏 ping。
The DNS server is down		DNS 服务器不可用
Use another DNS server (1.1.1.1, for example) - or restart the router, which is the DNS server here		换用其他 DNS 服务器（如 1.1.1.1），或重启路由器，因为这里的 DNS 由路由器提供
Addresses work. Names don't.		IP 地址能通，域名不行。
Compare ping 1.1.1.1 with ping example.com, then ask nslookup.		比较 ping 1.1.1.1 和 ping example.com，再用 nslookup 查询。
A hosts-file line sends one site to the wrong place		hosts 文件中的一行把某个网站指向了错误地址
Remove the line from the hosts file (Notepad as administrator)		删除 hosts 文件中的那一行（以管理员身份打开记事本）
nslookup asks the DNS server. ping uses Windows' own list first.		nslookup 查询 DNS 服务器，ping 会先查 Windows 本地记录。
Open the hosts file.		打开 hosts 文件。
An MTU black hole: big packets vanish		MTU 黑洞：大数据包消失了
Lower the MTU (e.g. 1400) or ask the provider - small packets pass, big ones are dropped silently		降低 MTU（例如 1400）或联系运营商：小包能通过，大包却被静默丢弃
Small things work. Big things hang.		小数据能传，大数据会卡住。
Try the don't-fragment ping at full size.		用完整大小的数据包尝试禁止分片的 ping。
A rogue DHCP server handed out the addresses		有未授权的 DHCP 服务器在分配地址
Find and unplug the device at that address - a second router or a misconfigured box giving out leases		找到该地址对应的设备并断开它：可能是第二台路由器，或错误分配租约的设备
Who gave you your router and DNS addresses?		你的路由器和 DNS 地址是谁分配的？
Look at ipconfig's DHCP server, then arp -a.		查看 ipconfig 中的 DHCP 服务器，再运行 arp -a。
Another device has the same IP address		另一台设备使用了相同的 IP 地址
Give the other device an address outside the DHCP range (or a reservation) - then renew		给另一台设备设置 DHCP 范围外的地址（或保留地址），然后续租
Something answers some of the time.		有时能收到响应，有时不能。
The event log keeps a note when Windows sees it.		Windows 检测到冲突时会写入事件日志。
The default gateway is set wrong		默认网关设置错误
Set the gateway to the real router (or switch the adapter back to automatic / DHCP)		将网关设为真正的路由器，或将网卡改回自动获取 / DHCP
The router you're told to use doesn't answer.		设置中指定的路由器没有响应。
Compare ipconfig's gateway with what arp -a sees.		比较 ipconfig 中的网关与 arp -a 检测到的设备。
DHCP failed - no address from the router		DHCP 失败：没有从路由器获取地址
Restart the router (or check its DHCP server is on), then renew the address		重启路由器，或检查 DHCP 服务是否开启，然后续租地址
Look at the address itself.		看看 IP 地址本身。
169.254 means Windows gave itself one.		169.254 表示 Windows 自行分配了地址。
A sign-in page (captive portal) is in the way		需要先通过网络登录页面（强制门户）
Open a browser to an http:// site and accept the network's sign-in page		用浏览器打开一个 http:// 网站，完成网络登录或确认条款
Pings work. The web shows something else.		Ping 正常，网页却显示其他内容。
Open the website and read what comes back.		打开网站，看看实际返回什么。
A proxy that doesn't exist is set		设置了不存在的代理
Turn the proxy off (Settings > Network > Proxy) - and check for adware that set it		关闭代理（设置 > 网络 > 代理），并检查是否有广告软件修改了设置
Everything answers - except the browser.		其他检查都有响应，只有浏览器不行。
Look at the proxy settings.		查看代理设置。
Weak, crowded Wi-Fi		Wi-Fi 信号弱且信道拥挤
Move closer, switch to 5 GHz, or use a cable		靠近路由器，切换到 5 GHz，或改用网线
Even the router is slow and drops pings.		连路由器都响应缓慢，并且 ping 丢包。
Read the Wi-Fi status.		查看 Wi-Fi 状态。
Lower the MTU		降低 MTU
Remove a line from the hosts file		删除 hosts 文件中的一行
Move closer to the Wi-Fi		靠近 Wi-Fi 路由器
Change the DNS server		更换 DNS 服务器
Turn off the proxy		关闭代理
Renew the DHCP lease		续租 DHCP 租约
Restart the PC		重启电脑
Change the Wi-Fi channel		更换 Wi-Fi 信道
The internet has been slow every night this week, and the data cap is nearly gone. Nobody at home stays up past midnight.		这周每天晚上网络都很慢，流量额度也快用完了。家里没有人熬夜到午夜以后。
Since this evening, sites are slow and some show certificate warnings. Every device is affected.		从今晚开始，网站变慢了，有些还出现证书警告。所有设备都受影响。
Some devices lost the internet after a new gadget arrived. Others are fine. Restarting fixes it - sometimes.		新设备接入后，部分设备无法上网，其他设备却正常。重启有时能解决。
Someone set up a device to look like part of the furniture. It's been busy.		有人把一台设备伪装成家具的一部分，而且它一直很忙。
Gaming has lagged in the evenings for two weeks. The Wi-Fi password was given to a 'friend of a friend' once.		游戏已经连续两周在晚上卡顿。Wi-Fi 密码曾给过一位“朋友的朋友”。
Two devices have private (random) hardware addresses. That alone is normal - phones do it.		两台设备使用了随机私有硬件地址。这本身很正常，手机通常会这样做。
One of them has been here every day for months. The other appeared 6 days ago.		其中一台几个月来每天都在，另一台是 6 天前才出现的。
The new one joins only between 1 and 5 in the morning - and moves the most data.		新设备只在凌晨 1 点到 5 点接入，而且传输的数据最多。
Look at which hardware address claims to be the router.		看看哪个硬件地址声称自己是路由器。
The router's real address starts f4:f2:6d (TP-Link).		真正路由器的地址以 f4:f2:6d 开头（TP-Link）。
One PC on the network now says 'I am the router' - every packet goes through it first.		网络中的一台电脑现在声称“我是路由器”，所有数据包都会先经过它。
Two DHCP servers answer. Only the router should.		有两台 DHCP 服务器响应，本应只有路由器响应。
Devices that take the second offer get the wrong gateway.		接受第二份地址分配的设备会获得错误的网关。
The second offer comes from a device that arrived yesterday.		第二份地址分配来自昨天才接入的设备。
There are two printers. You own one.		有两台打印机，但你只拥有一台。
A printer moving 10 GB? Printers barely use the network.		打印机传输了 10 GB？打印机通常很少使用网络。
Its maker (from the hardware address) isn't HP at all.		从硬件地址查到的制造商根本不是 HP。
The family has no Samsung phone.		家里没有 Samsung 手机。
It joins at 18:00 every day and leaves before midnight.		它每天 18:00 接入，午夜前离开。
It uploads for hours - uploads hurt game ping most.		它连续上传数小时，而上传最容易拉高游戏 ping。
A neighbour has the Wi-Fi password. Random MACs are normal, so the tell was the timing and the data, not the address. Change the Wi-Fi password and turn on WPA3 if the router has it.		邻居知道 Wi-Fi 密码。随机 MAC 很正常，真正的线索是接入时间和流量，而非地址。更改 Wi-Fi 密码；若路由器支持，再启用 WPA3。
ARP spoofing: a PC answered for the router's address so traffic flows through it (a man in the middle). Usually malware or a 'network tool' left running. Unplug it and scan it.		ARP 欺骗：一台电脑冒用路由器地址应答，让流量先经过自己，形成中间人攻击。通常是恶意软件或未关闭的网络工具。断开该设备并进行扫描。
A rogue DHCP server: a Raspberry Pi set up as a router answers before the real one. Turn off its DHCP server. Switches with DHCP snooping stop this at work.		未授权的 DHCP 服务器：一台被设为路由器的 Raspberry Pi 抢先响应。关闭它的 DHCP 服务。办公网络可用交换机的 DHCP snooping 功能防范。
A name is just a label anyone can type. The maker comes from the hardware address, and the data use gave it away. Block it in the router and change the Wi-Fi password.		设备名称只是任何人都能填写的标签。制造商由硬件地址推断，流量用量暴露了它。在路由器中屏蔽该设备，并更改 Wi-Fi 密码。
A guest who kept the password. Change it, and give visitors the router's guest network next time.		访客保留了密码。更改密码，下次让访客使用路由器的访客网络。
Opening a web connection		建立网页连接
The server can't acknowledge alone before it says SYN: the second step is one packet, SYN-ACK. Here a lone ACK came first.		服务器不能在发送 SYN 之前单独确认。第二步应为一个 SYN-ACK 包，这里却先出现了单独的 ACK。
SYN - let's talk, my sequence starts at 1000		SYN：开始通信，我的序列号从 1000 开始
ACK 1001 - got it		ACK 1001：收到了
SYN-ACK - fine, mine starts at 5000, ack 1001		SYN-ACK：好的，我的从 5000 开始，确认号为 1001
Getting an address (DHCP)		获取地址（DHCP）
DISCOVER - any DHCP server?		DISCOVER：有 DHCP 服务器吗？
REQUEST - I'll take 192.168.1.37		REQUEST：我要使用 192.168.1.37
OFFER - you can have 192.168.1.37		OFFER：可以分配给你 192.168.1.37
ACK - it's yours for 24 h		ACK：该地址归你使用 24 h
Finding a name (DNS)		查询域名（DNS）
The PC asked the router, but the answer came from 8.8.8.8. A real answer comes from the server you asked - this is how DNS spoofing looks.		电脑查询的是路由器，应答却来自 8.8.8.8。正常应答应来自被查询的服务器，这正是 DNS 欺骗的表现。
Query: where is example.com? (id 0x3a1f)		查询：example.com 在哪里？（id 0x3a1f）
Answer: 93.184.215.14 (id 0x3a1f)		应答：93.184.215.14（id 0x3a1f）
SYN to port 443		向端口 443 发送 SYN
A secure page (TLS)		安全网页（TLS）
The client always speaks first in TLS: ClientHello, then ServerHello. A server can't answer a question nobody asked.		TLS 总是由客户端先发起：先 ClientHello，再 ServerHello。服务器不能回答尚未提出的问题。
TCP handshake done		TCP 握手完成
ServerHello - let's use TLS 1.3, here's my key		ServerHello：使用 TLS 1.3，这是我的密钥
ClientHello - I speak TLS 1.3 and 1.2		ClientHello：我支持 TLS 1.3 和 1.2
Certificate, Finished		Certificate、Finished
A traceroute		一次路由追踪
With TTL 2 the packet dies at the second router, which says 'time exceeded'. 1.1.1.1 can't answer a packet that never reached it.		TTL 为 2 时，数据包在第二台路由器处失效，该路由器会返回“超时”。数据包没到 1.1.1.1，它就不可能应答。
Time exceeded (TTL ran out here)		超时（TTL 在此耗尽）
Time exceeded		超时
A big packet, don't fragment		大数据包，禁止分片
A router only sends 'fragmentation needed' when its next link is smaller than the packet. An MTU of 1600 would have carried 1500 bytes.		只有下一段链路容不下数据包时，路由器才会发送“需要分片”。MTU 为 1600 本应能承载 1500 字节。
1500-byte packet, don't fragment		1500 字节的数据包，禁止分片
ICMP: fragmentation needed, next-hop MTU 1600		ICMP：需要分片，下一跳 MTU 为 1600
1400-byte packet, don't fragment		1400 字节的数据包，禁止分片
Who has this address? (ARP)		谁拥有这个地址？（ARP）
A second device claims the router's address with its own hardware address, unasked - a gratuitous ARP spoof. The real router already answered.		第二台设备主动声称路由器地址对应自己的硬件地址，属于免费 ARP 欺骗。真正的路由器已经应答过。
ARP: who has 192.168.1.1? Tell 192.168.1.37		ARP：谁拥有 192.168.1.1？请告诉 192.168.1.37
ARP: 192.168.1.1 is at f4:f2:6d:4a:10:01		ARP：192.168.1.1 位于 f4:f2:6d:4a:10:01
ARP: 192.168.1.1 is at 00:d8:61:aa:bb:cc		ARP：192.168.1.1 位于 00:d8:61:aa:bb:cc
data to f4:f2:6d:4a:10:01		向 f4:f2:6d:4a:10:01 发送数据
Closing a connection		关闭连接
After a FIN the PC waits for the server's own FIN (and then TIME_WAIT) before reusing the same ports. A SYN on the same pair mid-close is wrong.		发送 FIN 后，电脑需要等待服务器的 FIN，再经过 TIME_WAIT，才能重用同一对端口。在关闭过程中向相同端口发送 SYN 是错误的。
FIN - I'm done sending		FIN：我已发送完毕
data: 4 KB		数据：4 KB
SYN - start a new connection on the same ports		SYN：使用相同端口建立新连接
A ping		一次 ping
A reply carries the same id as its request. id 9 belongs to someone else's ping - Windows would ignore it.		应答的 id 必须与请求一致。id 9 属于别人的 ping，Windows 会忽略它。
Echo request, id 7, seq 1		回显请求，id 7，seq 1
Echo reply, id 7, seq 1		回显应答，id 7，seq 1
Echo request, id 7, seq 2		回显请求，id 7，seq 2
Echo reply, id 9, seq 2		回显应答，id 9，seq 2
A UDP game packet		UDP 游戏数据包
UDP has no ACKs - nothing is acknowledged at the transport layer. A game that needs one builds it into its own messages.		UDP 没有 ACK，传输层不会确认收到数据。需要确认的游戏会在自己的消息中实现这一机制。
UDP game input (40 bytes)		UDP 游戏输入（40 字节）
UDP world update (900 bytes)		UDP 游戏世界更新（900 字节）
TCP ACK for the update		对此次更新的 TCP ACK
UDP game input		UDP 游戏输入
a web page over HTTPS		通过 HTTPS 访问网页
plain HTTP		明文 HTTP

mail from a mail app (submission)		邮件应用发送邮件（提交）

NTP (the clock)		NTP（时间同步）
a DHCP server		DHCP 服务器

SNMP traps		SNMP 陷阱
Windows file sharing (SMB)		Windows 文件共享（SMB）
RADIUS sign-in		RADIUS 登录
an iperf3 server		iperf3 服务器
STUN (calls finding their public address)		STUN（通话查询自身公网地址）
FTP (control)		FTP（控制连接）
mail between servers (SMTP)		服务器间的邮件传输（SMTP）
private (home or office)		私网（家庭或办公）
public (the internet)		公网（互联网）
loopback (this PC)		回环（本机）
IPv4 link-local (self-assigned)		IPv4 链路本地地址（自动分配）
shared by your provider (CGNAT)		运营商共享地址（CGNAT）
multicast (a group)		多播（一组设备）
documentation (examples only)		文档地址（仅作示例）
Which port:		使用哪个端口：
What kind of address is		此地址属于哪一类：
10 scenarios		10 个场景
5 cases		5 起案件
10 puzzles		10 道谜题
8 right to win		答对 8 题获胜
9 right to win		答对 9 题获胜
Your PC can't get to example.com. Everything below is a simulation - run the commands and find out why.		你的电脑无法访问 example.com。以下全是模拟，请运行命令找出原因。
hint used		已用提示
wrong guess		次猜错
wrong guesses		次猜错
the clock lost		已扣除
min to hints and guesses		分钟，用于提示和猜错

proxy settings		代理设置

open https://example.com		打开 https://example.com
Is the padlock OK?		安全锁正常吗？
Chain check (the ladder of trust)		证书链检查（信任链）
HTTPS check		HTTPS 检查
Locks and keys		锁与密钥
Hashes, keys, signatures, certificates and the padlock - why HTTPS works, and why it breaks.		哈希、密钥、签名、证书和安全锁：HTTPS 为什么安全，又为什么会出问题。
LOCKS AND KEYS - CERTIFICATES AND HTTPS		锁与密钥：证书与 HTTPS
Break the Adeebo Award		攻破 Adeebo Award
Mallory the hacker tries ten ways to fake an award card. Will each one work?		黑客 Mallory 尝试用十种方法伪造奖卡。每种方法能成功吗？
How a card's claim code is made - and checked:		奖卡的申领码如何生成和验证：
AdeeboMeter > AdeeboMeter: name + points + date, mixed with a secret key (HMAC-SHA256)		AdeeboMeter > AdeeboMeter：将姓名、积分、日期与秘密密钥混合计算（HMAC-SHA256）
AdeeboMeter > You: your card + its code		AdeeboMeter > 你：奖卡及申领码
You > Hall of Fame: the card		你 > 名人堂：提交奖卡
Hall of Fame > Hall of Fame: same mix, same key - same code? You're in		名人堂 > 名人堂：相同数据与密钥能算出相同申领码吗？一致就收录
It works		能成功
It fails		会失败
Right - this one gets through.		答对了，这种方法能通过。
Right - it fails.		答对了，它会失败。
Not quite - this one actually works.		不太对，这种方法其实可行。
Not quite - it fails.		不太对，它会失败。
Next attack		下一种攻击
See how you did		查看你的表现
You know the Adeebo Award inside out		你已经彻底了解 Adeebo Award
Mallory fooled you a few times		Mallory 骗过了你几次
The code stops the easy cheats. The honest limit of an offline app: points live on your own PC, so the Hall of Fame is for bragging rights, not prizes.		申领码能挡住简单作弊。离线应用也有局限：积分保存在你的电脑上，因此名人堂只是展示成绩，没有奖品。
Mallory opens Ada's award card in a picture editor and changes 512 points to 799.		Mallory 用图片编辑器打开 Ada 的奖卡，把 512 分改成 799 分。
The code was made from the name, the points and the date. Change any one of them and the Hall of Fame's own mix gives a different code - the card is turned away.		申领码由姓名、积分和日期生成。修改任意一项，名人堂重新计算的结果就会不同，因此奖卡会被拒绝。
She copies Ada's card exactly and types her own name over Ada's.		她完整复制 Ada 的奖卡，再把姓名改成自己的。
The name is inside the code too. A new name needs a new code, and only the secret key can make one.		姓名也参与申领码计算。新姓名需要新申领码，而生成它需要秘密密钥。
She guesses a claim code at random - lots of them.		她随机猜测大量申领码。
16 characters of base 32 is 80 bits: about 1.2 million billion billion codes. At a billion guesses a second that's some 38 million years - for one card.		16 位 base 32 字符相当于 80 比特，约有 1.2 百万亿亿种组合。即使每秒猜十亿次，为一张奖卡猜中也要约 38 百万年。
She builds AdeeboMeter herself from the open source code, to print her own codes.		她从开源代码自行构建 AdeeboMeter，想生成自己的申领码。
The key isn't in the public code. A home-made build has none, so its cards say 'unofficial build' and carry no claim code at all.		公开源码中没有密钥。自行构建的版本没有密钥，所以奖卡会标为“非官方构建”，而且完全没有申领码。
She collects ten real cards and their codes, to work out the key from them.		她收集十张真实奖卡及其申领码，试图推算密钥。
HMAC-SHA256 only runs one way: seeing what comes out tells you nothing about the key that went in. Ten cards or ten thousand.		HMAC-SHA256 是单向运算。知道输出结果，无法推算输入的密钥，十张或一万张奖卡都一样。
She sends Ada's real card again, to get Ada listed twice.		她再次提交 Ada 的真实奖卡，想让 Ada 被收录两次。
One entry per card and per name: the check answers 'already in the Hall of Fame' and adds nothing.		每张奖卡和每个姓名只收录一次。检查会返回“已在名人堂中”，不会重复添加。
She sends a card that claims 2,000 points.		她提交了一张声称有 2,000 分的奖卡。
There are only about 1,000 points to earn in all. An impossible total is turned away before the code is even checked.		总共只有大约 1,000 分可以获得。不可能的总分会在验证申领码前被拒绝。
She types <script>alert(1)</script> as her name, hoping the website runs it.		她将姓名填写为 <script>alert(1)</script>，希望网站执行它。
Names are cleaned to letters, digits and a few marks before anything else, and the page shows every field as plain text - never as code.		系统首先将姓名限制为字母、数字和少量符号。页面将所有字段显示为纯文本，绝不会当作代码执行。
She edits the points file on her own PC to 500, then makes a card the normal way.		她将自己电脑上的积分文件改成 500 分，再正常生成奖卡。
This one works - honestly. AdeeboMeter is offline and your points live on your PC, so the code proves the card came out of AdeeboMeter unchanged, not that every point was earned. That's why the Hall of Fame is for fun, not prizes.		这种方法确实可行。AdeeboMeter 离线运行，积分保存在你的电脑上。申领码只能证明奖卡由 AdeeboMeter 生成后未被修改，不能证明每一分都按规则获得。因此名人堂只供娱乐，没有奖品。
She takes the official AdeeboMeter apart with a decompiler, looking for the key.		她用反编译工具拆解官方 AdeeboMeter，寻找密钥。
With enough patience, yes: an app that makes codes offline has to carry its key somewhere inside. The code stops the easy cheats - an edited picture, a copied card - not a determined expert with time to spare.		只要足够有耐心，确实可以。离线生成申领码的应用必须将密钥放在内部某处。申领码能挡住改图、复制奖卡等简单作弊，挡不住有时间钻研的专家。
JUST FOR FUN		纯属娱乐

Eight broken networks, fifteen seconds each. Read the dots, call the cause. Faster scores more.		八个故障网络，每题十五秒。看状态点，判断原因。答得越快，得分越高。
6 right to win		答对 6 题获胜
The packet dungeon		数据包地牢
Eight rooms, from the cable to the vault. Three hearts. Each room you clear leaves a scroll.		八个房间，从网线走到宝库。三颗心，每过一关就能找到一张卷轴。
reach the treasure		找到宝藏
Your widget turns red. Fifteen seconds to say why. Right answers score 100, plus 10 for every second left. Six right survives.		小组件变红了。你有十五秒判断原因。答对得 100 分，每剩一秒加 10 分。答对六题就能过关。

Eight rooms stand between you and the treasure - from the cable at the bottom to the encrypted vault at the top. Each has a guardian with a riddle. A wrong answer costs a heart; lose all three and you start again.		你和宝藏之间有八个房间，从最底层的网线到最上层的加密宝库。每间都有出题的守卫。答错会失去一颗心，三颗心全失去就得重来。

Only when you press them.		通话前检查：向路由器和 1.1.1.1 各发 20 次 ping。DNS 竞速：向每台服务器进行 21 次域名查询（你的服务器、路由器、1.1.1.1、8.8.8.8、9.9.9.9，总计约 100 次）。走动测试：每个房间向路由器发 20 次 ping。只有点击时才运行。

 more min: public IP addresses (never private ones or names you typed) go to RIPEstat and PeeringDB to name each network on the path, its route and RPKI.		 分钟内：将公网 IP（绝不包含私网 IP 或你输入的名称）发送给 RIPEstat 和 PeeringDB，以标注沿途网络、路由与 RPKI。
Off. When on, Toolbox can name every network on your path (AS numbers), where they hand over, the destination's route and RPKI, and how the internet reaches you.		已关闭。开启后，工具箱可标注路径上的各个网络（AS 号）、网络交接点、目的地路由与 RPKI，以及互联网如何连接到你。
Only when a check you start fails: one tiny request to the same test page Windows uses, to catch hotel and airport sign-in pages.		仅在你主动发起的检查失败时，向 Windows 同款测试页面发一个轻量请求，用于识别酒店、机场的网络登录页面。
Off: a failed check won't look for a Wi-Fi sign-in page.		关闭：检查失败后不寻找 Wi-Fi 登录页面。
On: Chain check and HTTPS check also ask a certificate's issuer whether it was withdrawn (its revocation list or OCSP address - one extra connection, 10 s at most).		开启：证书链检查和 HTTPS 检查还会向签发机构查询证书是否撤销（CRL 或 OCSP 地址，额外连接一次，最多 10 s）。
Off. When on, Chain check and HTTPS check also ask a certificate's issuer whether it was withdrawn (revoked) - one extra connection to the issuer.		已关闭。开启后，证书链检查和 HTTPS 检查会额外连接签发机构一次，查询证书是否被撤销。
Reads names from Windows' own DNS cache - no network traffic.		从 Windows 自带的 DNS 缓存读取域名，不产生网络流量。
Off: connections show bare IP addresses.		关闭：连接中只显示 IP 地址。
Wi-Fi keeps dropping		Wi-Fi 频繁断线
Saved in your AppData folder. Nothing leaves this PC.		保存在你的 AppData 文件夹，内容不会离开本机。
Saved next to AdeeboMeter.exe. Nothing leaves this PC.		保存在 AdeeboMeter.exe 旁边，内容不会离开本机。
The lightest touch. Router every 30 s, internet every 5 min, Wi-Fi every 2 min (every 10 s while this window is open), no call watching.		最轻量：每 30 s 检查路由器，每 5 min 检查互联网，每 2 min 查询 Wi-Fi（此窗口打开时每 10 s），不监测通话。

Follows what you're doing.		跟随当前活动。
Bits (Mb/s)		比特（Mb/s）
Bytes (MB/s)		字节（MB/s）
Only when you run it: an MTR to the destinations you chose for the minutes you chose; a reverse name lookup for each router on the way (to your own DNS server); up to 4 page reads from a cable modem at 192.168.100.1 (its signal page); your public IP (one request to www.cloudflare.com/cdn-cgi/trace) only if you type yes.		仅主动运行时：按你选择的目的地和时长运行 MTR；向你的 DNS 服务器反查沿途各路由器名称；最多读取位于 192.168.100.1 的有线调制解调器信号页面 4 次；只有输入 yes 才会查询公网 IP（向 www.cloudflare.com/cdn-cgi/trace 请求一次）。
Check a destination sends only what you ask for, to where you ask, when you press Check - plus one DNS question for the same name to 1.1.1.1 (8.8.8.8 if that fails) - or, when an IT policy turns online lookups off, to your own DNS server, the second opinion that spots a filter. An IT policy with online_lookups = off sends it to your own DNS server instead.		“检查目的地”仅在点击检查时，向指定目标发送你要求的测试内容，并向 1.1.1.1 查询同一域名，失败则用 8.8.8.8，以第二份结果识别过滤。如果 IT 策略设定 online_lookups = off，则改向你自己的 DNS 服务器查询。
Listens to your own adapter only while you press Start. Nothing is sent; captures are deleted when AdeeboMeter exits.		仅在点击“开始”后监听你自己的网卡。不发送任何内容，退出 AdeeboMeter 时删除抓包。
Open this view		打开此视图
Click the tray icon, or press Ctrl+Alt+A from anywhere.		点击托盘图标，或在任意位置按 Ctrl+Alt+A。
Back to the corner		返回角落
Esc, minimise, the X or the Back to corner button - they all do the same thing: this view closes, gives its memory back, and the widget keeps watching.		Esc、最小化、X 和“返回角落”效果相同：关闭此视图并释放内存，小组件继续监测。
Quit completely		完全退出
Right-click the tray icon and choose Exit. Nothing keeps running - not even a helper.		右键点击托盘图标，选择“退出”。所有进程都会停止，包括辅助进程。
Learn anything		探索学习内容
Settings decides how much AdeeboMeter watches in the background - from Penny to Gamer.		在设置中选择 AdeeboMeter 的后台监测强度，从 Penny 到玩家模式。
Readable source		可阅读的源码
The whole app is plain source code you can read before you run it, built with the compiler that already ships inside Windows.		整个应用的源代码都可在运行前阅读，使用 Windows 自带的编译器构建。
No admin rights		无需管理员权限
Everything it shows comes from what Windows already lets a normal user see. If something is locked, it simply doesn't show.		所显示的信息均来自普通 Windows 用户已有的权限。若无权查看，就不显示。
Secure by default		默认安全
Checks use TLS with Windows' own certificate validation (Windows picks TLS 1.2 or 1.3) - it never skips a certificate check. Inputs are validated; no shell is ever used.		检查使用 TLS 和 Windows 自带证书验证（由 Windows 选择 TLS 1.2 或 1.3），绝不跳过证书检查。所有输入均验证，绝不调用 shell。
Light as a feather		轻巧运行
Around 70 small checks a minute on Balanced (about 8 on Penny) in the background. The heavy tools only run while this window is open, and always stop by themselves.		均衡模式下后台每分钟约 70 次轻量检查，Penny 约 8 次。重型工具仅在此窗口打开时运行，并会自行停止。
Leaves no trace		干净退出
Settings and a small log live next to the exe (an installed copy keeps them in your AppData folder). Captures are deleted on exit. Window titles and traffic content are never logged. History (a few days, on this PC only) is on unless you switch it off - and one click deletes it.		设置和小日志保存在 exe 旁边，安装版则保存在 AppData。退出时删除抓包，绝不记录窗口标题或流量内容。默认开启仅本机保存的数天历史，随时可关闭或一键删除。
Every conversation your PC is having right now - which app, with whom, on which port, in what state. New ones flash in, closed ones fade out. Hover a state to learn what it means; press Sniff on a row to record just that conversation for Wireshark.		查看电脑当前的每条通信：哪个应用、与谁、哪个端口、什么状态。新连接闪现，关闭的逐渐淡出。悬停状态可看解释；点击某行的 Sniff 只抓取该连接，供 Wireshark 分析。
Everything about your link: band, channel, signal in dBm, speeds, security, who else shares your air (with a channel map), addresses, errors, and how healthy the hop to your router is. Click the Wi-Fi dot to get here.		连接的完整信息：频段、信道、dBm 信号、速率、安全性、共享信道的邻居及信道图、地址、错误和到路由器这一跳的健康情况。点击 Wi-Fi 状态点即可进入。
Every program on your PC, in plain English: what it does, how it works, why it's there, what happens without it - and whether it's safe to close. AdeeboMeter only explains; it never closes anything.		用简单的话解释每个程序：作用、工作方式、为何存在、缺少它的影响，以及是否能安全关闭。AdeeboMeter 只解释，绝不替你关闭程序。
Type a name, an IP or a URL. AdeeboMeter walks the whole path like an engineer would - DNS, route, ping, TCP, TLS, HTTP, packet size, every router on the way - and tells you in one sentence where it breaks. It also tells the path's story: who carries your traffic, where networks hand over (that's where BGP decides), NAT, tunnels and firewalls. Pin the places you check often and run them all at once.		输入域名、IP 或网址。AdeeboMeter 像工程师一样检查 DNS、路由、ping、TCP、TLS、HTTP、包大小及沿途路由器，再用一句话指出问题位置。还会说明谁在传输流量、网络在何处交接（BGP 决策处）、NAT、隧道和防火墙。可固定常用目的地并一起测试。
A one-click check-up of the things that quietly cause trouble: days since restart, pending updates, disk space, adapter errors, crowded Wi-Fi, proxies, VPN, DNS, memory and power.		一键检查常见隐患：距重启天数、待更新项目、磁盘空间、网卡错误、Wi-Fi 拥挤、代理、VPN、DNS、内存和电源。
The diary. Every roam, drop, DNS change, VPN flap and app hiccup in the last two hours, with the time it happened - so 'it was slow at 2:15' finally has an answer.		网络日记：记录最近两小时的漫游、断线、DNS 变化、VPN 波动和应用异常，以及发生时间，让“2:15 时很慢”终于有迹可查。
Your own connection, one layer at a time: which route won and why, your router's hardware address, MTU and packet anatomy, the handshake as a ladder with the time of each step, and with Wireshark the real packets explained field by field. Plus Teams and Office end to end.		逐层查看你自己的连接：路由为何胜出、路由器硬件地址、MTU、数据包结构和每步计时的握手图。配合 Wireshark，可逐字段解读真实数据包，还能端到端了解 Teams 和 Office。
Who started whom (the process tree), what each svchost is hosting, what a program is doing right now (CPU, memory, disk, connections) and why it started, everything that starts with Windows - and the deep dives on the processes people ask about.		谁启动了谁（进程树）、每个 svchost 托管什么、程序当前的 CPU、内存、磁盘和连接活动、启动原因、所有开机启动项，以及常见进程的深入说明。
Learn (Ctrl+K)		学习（Ctrl+K）
Press Ctrl+K anywhere in this window to jump to a tab, a program, a term or a protocol. Learn mode explains each protocol with its ladder diagram and, with Wireshark, shows it live on your PC for ten seconds.		在此窗口任意位置按 Ctrl+K，跳转到标签页、程序、术语或协议。学习模式以时序图解释协议；安装 Wireshark 后，还能实时观察本机十秒钟的活动。
Wireshark's engine (tshark), only if you have Wireshark installed and only while you press Start. It shows what your traffic is made of, who you talk to by name, and Wireshark's own warnings. It stops by itself.		使用 Wireshark 引擎 tshark，仅在已安装 Wireshark 且点击“开始”后运行。显示流量组成、通信对象名称和 Wireshark 自身的警告，并会自行停止。
straight to Adeeb - no account needed		直接发给 Adeeb，无需账号
say what you did and what you expected - no account needed		说明操作和预期结果，无需账号
Source code on GitHub		GitHub 上的源码
everything AdeeboMeter does can be read there		可在此阅读 AdeeboMeter 的全部行为
Copied - paste it into the page that opened		已复制，请粘贴到刚打开的页面
When the internet feels slow, most of us open Task Manager, then a command prompt, then ping, then guess. AdeeboMeter does all of that for you - quietly, every second - and tells you in one plain sentence what's wrong and what to do about it.		网络感觉慢时，我们通常打开任务管理器、命令提示符，再运行 ping，最后靠猜。AdeeboMeter 每秒静默完成这些检查，再用一句简单的话告诉你哪里有问题、该怎么做。
The corner widget		角落小组件
A small card in the bottom-right corner, faded to 30% so it never distracts. It shows the one thing that matters - is everything OK - plus CPU, memory, disk and network at a glance. It never takes clicks, so whatever is underneath keeps working. When a window covers the corner it folds into a tiny chip; rest your mouse on it for half a second and it opens.		右下角的小卡片，默认不透明度为 30%，避免打扰。一眼查看整体是否正常，以及 CPU、内存、磁盘和网络。不会拦截点击，底下的内容照常可用。有窗口遮住角落时会折叠为小图标，悬停半秒即可展开。
Getting around		界面导航
Is it me or the network?		是电脑还是网络的问题？
The five dots at the top follow your traffic out of the building: your PC, your Wi-Fi or cable to the router, your provider (or VPN), DNS - the internet's phone book - and the internet itself. The first dot that isn't green is where the trouble starts. Everything before it is fine; everything after it is a victim, not a cause. That's the whole trick.		顶部五个状态点沿着流量路径排列：电脑、到路由器的 Wi-Fi 或网线、运营商或 VPN、DNS，以及互联网。第一个非绿色点就是问题起点。之前的环节正常，之后的环节只是受影响，并非原因。原理就是这么简单。

DNS - the internet's phone book		DNS：互联网的通讯录
You type a name; DNS finds the number.		你输入域名，DNS 找到对应地址。
What does DNS do when you type a site's name?		输入网站域名时，DNS 做什么？
Finds the IP address for that name		查找该域名的 IP 地址
Encrypts the page		加密网页
Picks the fastest Wi-Fi channel		选择最快的 Wi-Fi 信道
Gives your PC its own address		给电脑分配自己的地址
Turns a name like github.com into the number computers use.		将 github.com 这样的域名转换成电脑使用的数字地址。
Slow DNS makes every new site slow; broken DNS looks like 'no internet'.		DNS 慢会让新网站打开慢；DNS 故障则像是断网。
Where is github.com?		github.com 在哪里？
140.82.112.3 (good for 60 s)		140.82.112.3（有效期 60 s）

DHCP - how your PC gets an address		DHCP：电脑如何获得地址
Four messages, and you're on the network.		四条消息，让你接入网络。
What does DHCP give your PC when it joins a network?		电脑加入网络时，DHCP 提供什么？
An IP address, the router and DNS servers		IP 地址、路由器和 DNS 服务器
A Wi-Fi password		Wi-Fi 密码
A faster internet plan		更快的宽带套餐
The website's certificate		网站证书
Gives your PC an address, the router's address and DNS servers when you join a network.		加入网络时，为电脑提供自身地址、路由器地址和 DNS 服务器。
A 169.254.x.x address means DHCP failed - restart Wi-Fi or the router.		169.254.x.x 地址说明 DHCP 失败，可尝试重连 Wi-Fi 或重启路由器。
Everyone		所有设备
DISCOVER - any DHCP server here?		DISCOVER：这里有 DHCP 服务器吗？
REQUEST - I'll take .37		REQUEST：我选择 .37
ACK - it's yours for 24 hours		ACK：该地址归你使用 24 小时
Turn Wi-Fi off and on again.		关闭 Wi-Fi 再重新开启。
ARP - finding the hardware next door		ARP：查找身边设备的硬件地址
IP addresses for the journey, hardware addresses for the next step.		IP 地址用于全程寻址，硬件地址用于下一步转发。
What does ARP find out?		ARP 查找什么？
The hardware (MAC) address of a device on your own network		本地网络设备的硬件地址（MAC）
The country a server is in		服务器所在国家
The best route across the internet		跨互联网的最佳路由
Which app owns a port		哪个应用占用端口
Finds the hardware address of the router next to you, so packets can leave the room.		查找附近路由器的硬件地址，让数据包能离开本地网络。
If the router's hardware address suddenly changes, someone may be in the middle.		路由器硬件地址突然变化，可能有人在中间拦截流量。
Who has 192.168.1.1?		谁拥有 192.168.1.1？
I do - 3c:84:6a:..		是我：3c:84:6a:..
Wait - it happens on its own every few minutes.		等待即可，通常每隔几分钟自动发生。
TCP - the reliable conversation		TCP：可靠通信
Numbered, acknowledged, resent when lost.		编号、确认，丢失后重传。
What does TCP do when a packet goes missing?		数据包丢失时，TCP 怎么办？
Sends it again, so nothing arrives missing		重新发送，确保数据完整到达
Skips it to stay fast		跳过它，保持速度
Closes the connection		关闭连接
Asks DNS again		再次查询 DNS
A reliable conversation: numbered pieces, every one confirmed, lost ones sent again.		可靠的通信：数据分段编号，每段都确认，丢失的重新发送。
Lots of resends (retransmits) mean loss on the way - usually Wi-Fi.		重传很多意味着途中有丢包，常见于 Wi-Fi。
Server		服务器
SYN - let's talk		SYN：开始通信吧
SYN-ACK - sure		SYN-ACK：好的
ACK - connected		ACK：连接建立
data		数据
ACK - got it		ACK：收到了
Refresh a web page.		刷新网页。
UDP - fast and fire-and-forget		UDP：快速发送，不等确认
No handshake, no resending - speed first.		无需握手，不负责重传，速度优先。
Why do games and calls usually use UDP?		游戏和通话为什么通常使用 UDP？
Late data is useless, so speed beats resending		迟到的数据已无用，因此及时性比重传更重要
UDP is encrypted and TCP isn't		UDP 已加密，TCP 没有
UDP packets can't be lost		UDP 数据包不会丢失
Routers block TCP		路由器阻止 TCP
Fire and forget: no confirmations, no resends - fast, for calls, games and DNS.		发出即不再等待：没有确认和重传，速度快，适合通话、游戏和 DNS。
A lost UDP packet is just gone - in a call that's a glitch or robot voice.		丢失的 UDP 包不会自动补发，通话中可能表现为断音或机械音。
Join a Teams call or play a video.		加入 Teams 通话或播放视频。
packet 1		数据包 1
packet 2		数据包 2
packet 3 (no replies needed)		数据包 3（无需应答）
TLS - the padlock		TLS：安全锁
Proves who the server is, then encrypts everything.		先验证服务器身份，再加密后续通信。
What does the padlock (TLS) prove before encrypting?		加密前，安全锁（TLS）证明什么？
That the server really is who it says it is		服务器确实是它声称的身份
That your Wi-Fi is fast		你的 Wi-Fi 很快
That the site has no ads		网站没有广告
That your PC has no viruses		电脑没有病毒
The padlock: proves the server is real and encrypts everything after.		安全锁：验证服务器真实身份，然后加密后续内容。
A certificate error means the wrong server, a wrong clock, or something in the middle.		证书错误可能表示服务器不对、系统时间不对，或途中存在拦截。
ClientHello - I speak TLS 1.3		ClientHello：我支持 TLS 1.3
ServerHello + certificate		ServerHello + 证书
Finished - keys agreed		Finished：密钥协商完成
encrypted request		加密请求
encrypted answer		加密应答
Open a website.		打开网站。
QUIC and HTTP/3 - the modern web		QUIC 与 HTTP/3：现代网页通信
TCP and TLS rebuilt on top of UDP.		基于 UDP 重新构建的可靠传输与 TLS 加密。
What is QUIC built on?		QUIC 基于什么？
UDP, with TCP-like reliability and TLS built in		UDP，并内置类似 TCP 的可靠传输和 TLS
A new kind of cable		一种新型网线
ARP		ARP
The modern web: connection and encryption in one quick handshake, over UDP 443.		现代网页通信：通过 UDP 443，用一次快速握手完成连接与加密。
If UDP is blocked, browsers quietly fall back to TCP - slower, but it works.		若 UDP 被阻止，浏览器会自动回退到 TCP，虽然慢些，但仍能工作。
Open YouTube or Google in Edge or Chrome.		在 Edge 或 Chrome 中打开 YouTube 或 Google。
hello + keys (UDP 443)		握手问候 + 密钥（UDP 443）
hello + certificate		握手问候 + 证书
request, already encrypted		请求，已经加密

What does a 404 answer from a web server mean?		网页服务器返回 404 是什么意思？

Your password is wrong		你的密码错误
The connection is encrypted		连接已加密
How apps ask for things: GET this page, POST this form - the server answers with a code.		应用请求资源的方式：GET 获取网页，POST 提交表单，服务器用状态码应答。
Codes: 200 fine, 401/403 not allowed, 404 not found, 5xx the server is broken.		状态码：200 正常，401/403 无权限，404 未找到，5xx 服务器故障。
GET /		GET /
200 OK + the page		200 OK + 网页

Are you there? How far? Which way?		你在线吗？有多远？走哪条路？
What does traceroute show?		路由追踪显示什么？

IPv6 and how your PC gets its addresses		IPv6 与电脑的地址获取
Many addresses at once, and some of them change every day.		同时拥有多个地址，其中部分每天变化。
Why can a PC have several IPv6 addresses at once?		电脑为什么能同时有多个 IPv6 地址？
Some are temporary (privacy) addresses that change over time		部分是随时间变化的临时隐私地址
IPv6 is broken		IPv6 出故障了
Each app gets its own		每个应用分配一个
One per Wi-Fi channel		每个 Wi-Fi 信道分配一个
The newer, much bigger address system; your PC usually has both IPv4 and IPv6.		更新、更大的地址系统，电脑通常同时拥有 IPv4 和 IPv6。
Broken IPv6 makes some sites slow to start while the PC falls back to IPv4.		IPv6 故障可能让部分网站启动慢，因为电脑需要等待回退到 IPv4。

NAT - many devices, one public address		NAT：多个设备，一个公网地址
Why your PC's address isn't the one the internet sees.		为什么电脑自身的地址不同于互联网看到的地址。
Why does a website see a different address than your PC's own?		网站为什么看到的地址与电脑自身不同？

Your PC lies about it		电脑隐瞒了它
TLS hides it		TLS 隐藏了它
Your router swaps your private address for its one public address on the way out.		发往外网时，路由器将你的私网地址换成自己的公网地址。
A 100.64.x.x hop means your provider adds a second NAT - some games and calls struggle.		出现 100.64.x.x 这一跳，说明运营商又加了一层 NAT，部分游戏和通话可能受影响。

MTU - how big a packet can be		MTU：数据包能有多大
1500 bytes, minus whatever tunnels add.		1500 字节，减去隧道所需的额外空间。
What usually makes the usable MTU smaller than 1500 bytes?		什么通常会让可用 MTU 小于 1500 字节？
A tunnel such as a VPN or PPPoE adding its own headers		VPN 或 PPPoE 等隧道增加了自身报头
A slow Wi-Fi signal		Wi-Fi 信号慢
Too many browser tabs		浏览器标签页太多
A weak password		密码太弱
The biggest packet that fits on a link - usually 1500 bytes, less on VPNs and PPPoE.		链路能容纳的最大包大小，通常为 1500 字节，VPN 和 PPPoE 下会更小。
Pages that half-load or hang on a VPN are often an MTU problem.		VPN 中网页加载不完整或卡住，常常与 MTU 有关。
1500 bytes		1500 字节
too big - needs 1400		过大，需要 1400
resends smaller		用更小的包重传

The layers, in plain words		用简单的话解释网络分层
A letter in envelopes inside envelopes - each layer adds one, the other side opens them in reverse.		像一封信套着多层信封，每层加一个，对端再按相反顺序拆开。
In the 'envelopes' picture of the layers, what does the receiving side do?		用“信封”比喻网络分层时，接收端会怎么做？
Opens the envelopes in reverse order		按相反顺序拆开信封
Adds more envelopes		再套更多信封
Throws away the outer one only		只丢掉最外层
Nothing		什么都不做
Each layer adds an envelope on the way out; the other side opens them in reverse.		发送时每层套一层信封，对端按相反顺序拆开。
The layer that fails tells you who to blame: room, router, firewall, or the website.		哪层失败，就提示问题在何处：本地、路由器、防火墙或网站。

Speed isn't one number		网速不只是一个数字
Bandwidth, delay, jitter and loss - four different things that all feel like "slow".		带宽、延迟、抖动和丢包，四种不同的问题都会让人觉得“慢”。

A big hard disk		大容量硬盘
A bright screen		明亮的屏幕
A long password		很长的密码
Bandwidth is how much; delay is how long; jitter is how uneven; loss is how much goes missing.		带宽是多少数据，延迟是等待多久，抖动是时间多不稳定，丢包是丢了多少。
Speed tests show bandwidth, but calls and browsing suffer from delay, jitter and loss.		测速显示带宽，但通话和浏览还会受延迟、抖动与丢包影响。

Path MTU - the biggest packet that fits		路径 MTU：能通过的最大数据包
Every link has a size limit; the smallest one on the way wins.		每段链路都有大小上限，沿途最小值决定全程上限。
Which link decides the biggest packet that fits a whole path?		哪一段链路决定整条路径能通过的最大包？
The smallest MTU anywhere on the way		沿途最小的 MTU
The first link		第一段链路
The fastest link		最快的链路
The last link		最后一段链路
Ethernet carries 1500 bytes; PPPoE, VPNs and tunnels wrap packets and leave less room.		以太网可承载 1500 字节，PPPoE、VPN 和隧道增加封装，留给原始包的空间更小。
Too-big packets are dropped - if no router says so, pages half-load and VPNs stall (a black hole).		过大的包会被丢弃；若路由器又不返回错误，网页会加载不全，VPN 会卡住，形成黑洞。
1500 bytes, don't fragment		1500 字节，禁止分片

Your packets and the replies often take different roads.		你发出的包和返回的应答，常常走不同道路。
Do replies always come back the same way your packets went?		应答总沿着请求的原路返回吗？

Each network sends traffic out by its own choice, so the reply route is decided by the far end.		各网络自行决定流量出口，因此返回路由由远端决定。
Loss or delay only on the way back looks like a problem 'out there' that no trace from your PC shows.		只在返程出现的丢包或延迟，看起来像远处有问题，但从本机发起的路由追踪可能看不到。

Why a PC feels slow		电脑为什么感觉慢
It's rarely the processor - it's memory and the disk.		通常不是处理器，而是内存和磁盘。
What usually makes a PC feel slow?		什么通常会让电脑感觉慢？
Running out of memory, so it waits on the disk		内存不足，导致等待磁盘读取
A slow processor clock		处理器时钟慢
A small screen		屏幕小
Too many desktop icons		桌面图标太多
When memory is full, Windows parks parts of programs on the disk and reads them back when needed - thousands of times slower.		内存满时，Windows 将程序的一部分暂存到磁盘，需要时再读回，速度会慢上数千倍。
Many pages read back per second, or a disk taking more than 15 ms per request, makes everything stutter.		每秒大量页面读回，或磁盘单次请求超过 15 ms，都会让操作卡顿。
